 ## Project structure
 
-- The global header/nav lives in `src/components/layout/AppHeader.tsx`, rendered from `__root.tsx`; nav entries come from `nav-links.ts` so adding a page means adding one entry there plus its route file.
+- The dev dashboard header/nav lives in `src/components/layout/AppHeader.tsx`, rendered from `__root.tsx` ONLY on `/workflow` and `/errors`; nav entries come from `nav-links.ts` (`Workflow & Boot-Up` and `Errors & Issues`). Customer-facing Plasmic routes (`/`, catch-all Plasmic pages) render cleanly with zero dev header clutter, and `/plasmic-host` renders ONLY `<PlasmicCanvasHost />`.
 - Every top-level page wraps its content in `PageShell` for a consistent title block and layout.
 - The app is dark-only: dark tokens live on `:root` in `src/styles.css` and `<html>` carries the `dark` class, so no theme toggle is needed.
+
+## ECMD PEN v4.2 (Antigravity `agy` v1.5.15) & Dual-AI Guardrails
+
+- **Admin & Dev Engine ΓÇö Antigravity CLI (`agy`), `-p` Last-Flag Rule & Checksum Diffing:** Active via `epen` -> `C:\ECMD\scripts\Start-EcmdPen.ps1` -> `C:\ECMD\scripts\run-pen-task.mjs` spawning `agy.exe --dangerously-skip-permissions --output-format stream-json --add-dir C:\ECMD -p "Execute the complete engineering task piped via stdin above."`. **CRITICAL:** `-p "<instruction>"` MUST ALWAYS be the VERY LAST elements of the args array when piping tasks via stdin (never put `-p`/`--print` before `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, or `agy` consumes the next flag as the prompt!). Runner preserves recursive `walkForToolsAndText` NDJSON logging, filters `root agent idle; waiting for...` as `AGY ORCHESTRATOR` (`isWarn = false`), computes pre/post-turn SHA-1 workspace checksum snapshots so `filesChanged` (saved to Supabase `public.ecmd_pen_turns` and lightweight `public/ai-telemetry.json`, with obsolete static dumps removed) records ONLY files actually created/modified/deleted during THAT turn, and auto-runs `node C:\ECMD\scripts\sync-ai-mirror.mjs --push` non-fatally after `npx tsc --noEmit` & `npx vitest run` succeed. Personal `@gmail.com` Google OAuth (`$0.00/turn`, Gemini 4). Strictly for owner/admin local code engineering, Plasmic components, SQL migrations, and internal ops. **NEVER wire `agy` or personal OAuth to public website visitors** (Google ToS account-ban & security boundary).
+- **Architect-Here / Fast-Insert-In-`agy` Workflow & CLI Model/Account Notes:** Use the Chrome sidebar to design exact schemas, data rows, and targeted code edits upfront, then have `agy` (`agy.exe` via `epen`) apply the exact edits/inserts, run `node scripts/ecmd-sql.mjs`, and run verification (`npx tsc --noEmit` & `npx vitest run`)ΓÇökeeping turns tightly scoped to conserve `agy` quota (`--effort medium` / `gemini-3.8-flash` when appropriate). Users can check available models with `agy models`, set `--effort` (`low|medium|high|xhigh|max`), or use interactive `/usage` and `/logout` when managing CLI auth/session state.
+- **Non-Interactive Shell Guard (`-UseBasicParsing` / Node `fetch`):** NEVER run PowerShell `Invoke-WebRequest` or `iwr` without `-UseBasicParsing` ΓÇö prefer Node `fetch()` (with `AbortSignal.timeout(8000)`) or `curl.exe -s` for all HTTP checks so non-interactive stdin NEVER hangs on PowerShell's Security Warning prompt.
+- **Public AI Mirror Primary Grounding (`mlhammes2/ecmd-ai-mirror`) & 26-CSV Wix Importer:** Chrome Gemini Sidebar & external architects use `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mlhammes2/ecmd-ai-mirror/main/SITE_MANIFEST.md` (and `SITE_MANIFEST.json`) as the primary behind-the-scenes codebase + Wix Velo vault + Supabase grounding source across `hub/`, `runner/` (excluding raw `wix-cms-exports/`), and `supabase-mirror/` (public schema DDL/JSON, 14 safe public Wix CMS/package/inclusions/button/slot/config/blueprint table snapshots with 12 RLS-protected private/PII Wix tables strictly excluded via `EXPLICIT_BLOCKED_TABLES`, edge function metadata, & registered Plasmic components), fetching exact raw GitHub URLs on demand alongside 0ms `http://localhost:8080/errors` (`#ecmd-unified-errors-json`). Built & scrubbed by `C:\ECMD\scripts\sync-ai-mirror.mjs` (zero `.env*`, mandatory secret redaction, zero auth/PII tables). +function cleanTokenRefsObj(refs?: ThemeStateTokenRefs): ThemeStateTokenRefs | undefined {
+  if (!refs) return undefined;
+  const next: ThemeStateTokenRefs = {};
+  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(refs)) {
+    if (typeof v === "string" && v.trim()) {
+      next[k as ThemeTokenAttrKey] = v.trim();
+    }
+  }
+  return Object.keys(next).length > 0 ? next : undefined;
+}
+
+export function bindTokenToGroupState(
+  group: DynamicGroupItem,
+  stateKey: GroupStateKey,
+  attr: ThemeTokenAttrKey,
+  tokenId: string
+): DynamicGroupItem {
+  if (stateKey === "default") {
+    const nextRefs = cleanTokenRefsObj({ ...(group.tokenRefs || {}), [attr]: tokenId });
+    return { ...group, tokenRefs: nextRefs };
+  }
+  const prevOv = group.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+  const nextRefs = cleanTokenRefsObj({ ...(prevOv.tokenRefs || {}), [attr]: tokenId });
+  return {
+    ...group,
+    stateOverrides: {
+      ...(group.stateOverrides || {}),
+      [stateKey]: { ...prevOv, tokenRefs: nextRefs }
+    }
+  };
+}
+
+function applyResolvedTokenSnapshotToGroupState(
+  group: DynamicGroupItem,
+  stateKey: GroupStateKey,
+  attr: ThemeTokenAttrKey,
+  token: ThemeDesignToken | undefined
+): DynamicGroupItem {
+  const removeRef = (refs?: ThemeStateTokenRefs): ThemeStateTokenRefs | undefined => {
+    if (!refs) return undefined;
+    const copy = { ...refs };
+    delete copy[attr];
+    return cleanTokenRefsObj(copy);
+  };
+
+  if (stateKey === "default") {
+    const next: DynamicGroupItem = { ...group, tokenRefs: removeRef(group.tokenRefs) };
+    if (!token) return next;
+    const clCss = compileDesignTokenCssValue(token, "cl");
+    const msCss = compileDesignTokenCssValue(token, "ms");
+    if (attr === "background") {
+      next.clBg = clCss;
+      next.msBg = msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "text") {
+      next.clText = clCss;
+      next.msText = msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "border") {
+      const clCfg = resolveDesignTokenBorderConfig(token, "cl");
+      const msCfg = resolveDesignTokenBorderConfig(token, "ms");
+      if (clCfg?.color !== undefined) next.clBorder = clCfg.color;
+      if (msCfg?.color !== undefined) next.msBorder = msCfg.color;
+      if (token.type === "border" && clCfg) {
+        if (clCfg.width !== undefined) next.borderWidth = clCfg.width;
+        if (clCfg.style !== undefined) next.borderStyle = clCfg.style;
+        if (clCfg.borderMode !== undefined) next.borderMode = clCfg.borderMode;
+        if (clCfg.borderNone !== undefined) next.borderNone = clCfg.borderNone;
+        if (clCfg.radius !== undefined && !next.tokenRefs?.borderRadius) next.radius = clCfg.radius;
+      }
+    } else if (attr === "borderRadius") {
+      next.radius = clCss || msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "boxShadow") {
+      next.clOuterShadow = clCss;
+      next.clShadow = clCss;
+      next.msOuterShadow = msCss;
+      next.msShadow = msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "innerShadow") {
+      next.clInnerShadow = clCss;
+      next.msInnerShadow = msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "textShadow") {
+      const clTs = resolveDesignTokenTextShadowConfig(token, "cl");
+      const msTs = resolveDesignTokenTextShadowConfig(token, "ms");
+      if (clTs) next.clTextShadow = clTs;
+      if (msTs) next.msTextShadow = msTs;
+    }
+    return next;
+  }
+
+  const prevOv = group.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+  const nextOv: GroupStateOverride = {
+    ...prevOv,
+    tokenRefs: removeRef(prevOv.tokenRefs),
+    cl: prevOv.cl ? { ...prevOv.cl } : undefined,
+    ms: prevOv.ms ? { ...prevOv.ms } : undefined
+  };
+  const nextGroup: DynamicGroupItem = { ...group };
+  if (token) {
+    const clCss = compileDesignTokenCssValue(token, "cl");
+    const msCss = compileDesignTokenCssValue(token, "ms");
+    if (attr === "background") {
+      nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), bg: clCss };
+      nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), bg: msCss };
+      if (stateKey === "hover") { nextGroup.clHoverBg = clCss; nextGroup.msHoverBg = msCss; }
+      if (stateKey === "selected") { nextGroup.clSelBg = clCss; nextGroup.msSelBg = msCss; }
+    } else if (attr === "text") {
+      nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), text: clCss };
+      nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), text: msCss };
+      if (stateKey === "hover") { nextGroup.clHoverText = clCss; nextGroup.msHoverText = msCss; }
+      if (stateKey === "selected") { nextGroup.clSelText = clCss; nextGroup.msSelText = msCss; }
+    } else if (attr === "border") {
+      const clCfg = resolveDesignTokenBorderConfig(token, "cl");
+      const msCfg = resolveDesignTokenBorderConfig(token, "ms");
+      if (clCfg?.color !== undefined) nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), border: clCfg.color };
+      if (msCfg?.color !== undefined) nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), border: msCfg.color };
+      if (stateKey === "focus") {
+        if (clCfg?.color !== undefined) nextGroup.clFocusBorder = clCfg.color;
+        if (msCfg?.color !== undefined) nextGroup.msFocusBorder = msCfg.color;
+      }
+      if (token.type === "border" && clCfg) {
+        if (clCfg.width !== undefined) nextOv.borderWidth = clCfg.width;
+        if (clCfg.style !== undefined) nextOv.borderStyle = clCfg.style;
+        if (clCfg.borderMode !== undefined) nextOv.borderMode = clCfg.borderMode;
+        if (clCfg.borderNone !== undefined) nextOv.borderNone = clCfg.borderNone;
+        if (clCfg.radius !== undefined && !nextOv.tokenRefs?.borderRadius) nextOv.radius = clCfg.radius;
+      }
+    } else if (attr === "borderRadius") {
+      nextOv.radius = clCss || msCss;
+    } else if (attr === "boxShadow") {
+      nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), outerShadow: clCss };
+      nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), outerShadow: msCss };
+    } else if (attr === "innerShadow") {
+      nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), innerShadow: clCss };
+      nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), innerShadow: msCss };
+    } else if (attr === "textShadow") {
+      const clTs = resolveDesignTokenTextShadowConfig(token, "cl");
+      const msTs = resolveDesignTokenTextShadowConfig(token, "ms");
+      if (clTs) nextOv.cl = { ...(nextOv.cl || {}), ...clTs };
+      if (msTs) nextOv.ms = { ...(nextOv.ms || {}), ...msTs };
+    }
+  }
+  nextGroup.stateOverrides = {
+    ...(group.stateOverrides || {}),
+    [stateKey]: nextOv
+  };
+  return nextGroup;
+}
+
+export function detachTokenFromGroupState(
+  group: DynamicGroupItem,
+  stateKey: GroupStateKey,
+  attr: ThemeTokenAttrKey,
+  tokens: ThemeDesignToken[]
+): DynamicGroupItem {
+  const tokenId =
+    stateKey === "default"
+      ? group.tokenRefs?.[attr]
+      : group.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]?.tokenRefs?.[attr];
+  const token = findDesignTokenById(tokens, tokenId);
+  return applyResolvedTokenSnapshotToGroupState(group, stateKey, attr, token);
+}
+
+export function deleteDesignTokenWithGroupFallback(
+  payload: DynamicGroupsPayload,
+  tokenId: string
+): DynamicGroupsPayload {
+  const tokens = hydrateThemeDesignTokens(payload.designTokens);
+  const targetToken = findDesignTokenById(tokens, tokenId);
+  const subStates: SubGroupStateKey[] = ["hover", "focus", "selected", "selectedHover", "disabled"];
+  const nextGroups: Record<string, DynamicGroupItem> = {};
+
+  for (const [k, rawGroup] of Object.entries(payload.groups || {})) {
+    if (!rawGroup) continue;
+    let updated = rawGroup;
+    if (updated.tokenRefs) {
+      for (const [attr, refId] of Object.entries(updated.tokenRefs)) {
+        if (refId === tokenId) {
+          updated = applyResolvedTokenSnapshotToGroupState(
+            updated,
+            "default",
+            attr as ThemeTokenAttrKey,
+            targetToken
+          );
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    if (updated.stateOverrides) {
+      for (const st of subStates) {
+        const refs = updated.stateOverrides?.[st]?.tokenRefs;
+        if (!refs) continue;
+        for (const [attr, refId] of Object.entries(refs)) {
+          if (refId === tokenId) {
+            updated = applyResolvedTokenSnapshotToGroupState(
+              updated,
+              st,
+              attr as ThemeTokenAttrKey,
+              targetToken
+            );
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    nextGroups[k] = updated;
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...payload,
+    groups: nextGroups,
+    designTokens: deleteThemeDesignToken(tokens, tokenId)
+  };
+}
+
+function cleanVal(v?: string): string {
+  return typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "";
+}
+
+export function hydrateLegacyStateOverrides(group: DynamicGroupItem): DynamicGroupItem {
+  const rawAny = group as any;
+  const existing = group.stateOverrides || {};
+  const nextOverrides: Partial<Record<SubGroupStateKey, GroupStateOverride>> = {
+    ...existing
+  };
+  const gDis = group.disabledProps || {};
+
+  // Hover hydration from legacy flat clHoverBg/msHoverBg/clHoverText/msHoverText/hoverBg/hoverText
+  const clHBg = cleanVal(group.clHoverBg ?? rawAny.hoverBg);
+  const msHBg = cleanVal(group.msHoverBg ?? rawAny.hoverBg);
+  const clHTxt = cleanVal(group.clHoverText ?? rawAny.hoverText);
+  const msHTxt = cleanVal(group.msHoverText ?? rawAny.hoverText);
+  if (clHBg || msHBg || clHTxt || msHTxt || existing.hover) {
+    const prevH = existing.hover || {};
+    const prevHDis: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(prevH.disabledProps || {}) };
+    if (gDis["hoverBg"] && prevHDis["bg"] === undefined) prevHDis["bg"] = true;
+    if (gDis["hoverText"] && prevHDis["text"] === undefined) prevHDis["text"] = true;
+    nextOverrides.hover = {
+      ...prevH,
+      cl: {
+        ...(prevH.cl || {}),
+        bg: prevH.cl?.bg !== undefined ? prevH.cl.bg : (clHBg || undefined),
+        text: prevH.cl?.text !== undefined ? prevH.cl.text : (clHTxt || undefined)
+      },
+      ms: {
+        ...(prevH.ms || {}),
+        bg: prevH.ms?.bg !== undefined ? prevH.ms.bg : (msHBg || undefined),
+        text: prevH.ms?.text !== undefined ? prevH.ms.text : (msHTxt || undefined)
+      },
+      disabledProps: Object.keys(prevHDis).length > 0 ? prevHDis : undefined
+    };
+  }
+
+  // Selected hydration from legacy flat clSelBg/msSelBg/clSelText/msSelText/selectedBg/selectedText
+  const clSBg = cleanVal(group.clSelBg ?? rawAny.selectedBg);
+  const msSBg = cleanVal(group.msSelBg ?? rawAny.selectedBg);
+  const clSTxt = cleanVal(group.clSelText ?? rawAny.selectedText);
+  const msSTxt = cleanVal(group.msSelText ?? rawAny.selectedText);
+  if (clSBg || msSBg || clSTxt || msSTxt || existing.selected) {
+    const prevS = existing.selected || {};
+    const prevSDis: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(prevS.disabledProps || {}) };
+    if (gDis["selBg"] && prevSDis["bg"] === undefined) prevSDis["bg"] = true;
+    if (gDis["selText"] && prevSDis["text"] === undefined) prevSDis["text"] = true;
+    nextOverrides.selected = {
+      ...prevS,
+      cl: {
+        ...(prevS.cl || {}),
+        bg: prevS.cl?.bg !== undefined ? prevS.cl.bg : (clSBg || undefined),
+        text: prevS.cl?.text !== undefined ? prevS.cl.text : (clSTxt || undefined)
+      },
+      ms: {
+        ...(prevS.ms || {}),
+        bg: prevS.ms?.bg !== undefined ? prevS.ms.bg : (msSBg || undefined),
+        text: prevS.ms?.text !== undefined ? prevS.ms.text : (msSTxt || undefined)
+      },
+      disabledProps: Object.keys(prevSDis).length > 0 ? prevSDis : undefined
+    };
+  }
+
+  // Focus hydration from legacy flat clFocusBorder/msFocusBorder/focusBorder
+  const clFBrd = cleanVal(group.clFocusBorder ?? rawAny.focusBorder);
+  const msFBrd = cleanVal(group.msFocusBorder ?? rawAny.focusBorder);
+  if (clFBrd || msFBrd || existing.focus) {
+    const prevF = existing.focus || {};
+    const prevFDis: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(prevF.disabledProps || {}) };
+    if (gDis["focusBorder"] && prevFDis["border"] === undefined) prevFDis["border"] = true;
+    nextOverrides.focus = {
+      ...prevF,
+      cl: {
+        ...(prevF.cl || {}),
+        border: prevF.cl?.border !== undefined ? prevF.cl.border : (clFBrd || undefined)
+      },
+      ms: {
+        ...(prevF.ms || {}),
+        border: prevF.ms?.border !== undefined ? prevF.ms.border : (msFBrd || undefined)
+      },
+      disabledProps: Object.keys(prevFDis).length > 0 ? prevFDis : undefined
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...group,
+    stateOverrides: Object.keys(nextOverrides).length > 0 ? nextOverrides : undefined
+  };
+}
+
+export function hasStateOverrides(group: DynamicGroupItem | null | undefined, stateKey: GroupStateKey): boolean {
+  if (!group) return false;
+  if (stateKey === "default") {
+    return Boolean(
+      cleanVal(group.clBg) ||
+      cleanVal(group.msBg) ||
+      cleanVal(group.clText) ||
+      cleanVal(group.msText) ||
+      cleanVal(group.clBorder) ||
+      cleanVal(group.msBorder) ||
+      cleanVal(group.radius) ||
+      cleanVal(group.borderWidth) ||
+      cleanVal(group.borderStyle) ||
+      group.borderNone ||
+      group.borderMode === "none" ||
+      cleanVal(group.fontWeight) ||
+      cleanVal(group.clOuterShadow) ||
+      cleanVal(group.msOuterShadow) ||
+      cleanVal(group.clInnerShadow) ||
+      cleanVal(group.msInnerShadow) ||
+      group.clTextShadow?.textShadowEnabled ||
+      group.msTextShadow?.textShadowEnabled ||
+      group.textShadowEnabled ||
+      Boolean(group.tokenRefs && Object.keys(group.tokenRefs).length > 0)
+    );
+  }
+  const ov = group.stateOverrides?.[stateKey];
+  if (!ov) return false;
+  const themeHas = (t?: GroupStateThemeValues) =>
+    Boolean(
+      cleanVal(t?.bg) ||
+      cleanVal(t?.text) ||
+      cleanVal(t?.border) ||
+      cleanVal(t?.icon) ||
+      cleanVal(t?.outerShadow) ||
+      cleanVal(t?.innerShadow) ||
+      t?.textShadowEnabled
+    );
+  return Boolean(
+    themeHas(ov.cl) ||
+    themeHas(ov.ms) ||
+    ov.textShadowEnabled ||
+    cleanVal(ov.borderWidth) ||
+    cleanVal(ov.borderStyle) ||
+    ov.borderNone ||
+    ov.borderMode === "none" ||
+    cleanVal(ov.fontWeight) ||
+    cleanVal(ov.radius) ||
+    cleanVal(ov.opacity) ||
+    ov.cursorNotAllowed ||
+    Boolean(ov.tokenRefs && Object.keys(ov.tokenRefs).length > 0)
+  );
+}

+export function createGroupWithAttributeProfile(
+  slotId: number,
+  name: string,
+  profile?: AttributeProfile | null,
+  prev?: Partial<DynamicGroupItem>
+): DynamicGroupItem {
+  const profileDisabled = attributeProfileToDisabledProps(profile);
+  return {
+    id: slotId,
+    enabled: true,
+    name,
+    selector: prev?.selector && prev.selector.trim() ? prev.selector : (".gm-group-" + slotId),
+    radius: prev?.radius || "",
+    spacing: prev?.spacing ?? false,
+    padding: prev?.padding || "",
+    margin: prev?.margin || "",
+    gap: prev?.gap || "",
+    clBg: prev?.clBg || "",
+    clText: prev?.clText || "",
+    clBorder: prev?.clBorder || "",
+    clIcon: prev?.clIcon || "",
+    clSelBg: prev?.clSelBg || "",
+    clSelText: prev?.clSelText || "",
+    msBg: prev?.msBg || "",
+    msText: prev?.msText || "",
+    msBorder: prev?.msBorder || "",
+    msIcon: prev?.msIcon || "",
+    msSelBg: prev?.msSelBg || "",
+    msSelText: prev?.msSelText || "",
+    attributeProfileId: profile?.id,
+    disabledProps: profileDisabled
+  };
 }

+    if (!cur) return;
+    const nextOverrides = { ...(cur.stateOverrides || {}) };
+    delete nextOverrides[stateKey];
+    const patch: Partial<DynamicGroupItem> = {
+      stateOverrides: Object.keys(nextOverrides).length > 0 ? nextOverrides : undefined
+    };
+    if (stateKey === "hover") {
+      patch.clHoverBg = ""; patch.msHoverBg = ""; patch.clHoverText = ""; patch.msHoverText = "";
+    } else if (stateKey === "selected") {
+      patch.clSelBg = ""; patch.msSelBg = ""; patch.clSelText = ""; patch.msSelText = "";
+    } else if (stateKey === "focus") {
+      patch.clFocusBorder = ""; patch.msFocusBorder = "";
+    }
+    updateSingleGroup(id, patch);
+  };
+
+  const applyProfileToSingleGroup = (id: number, profileId: string) => {
+    if (!profileId) return;
+    const cur = state.groups[String(id)];
+    const profile = attrStore.profiles.find((p) => p.id === profileId);
+    if (!cur || !profile) return;
+    updateSingleGroup(id, {
+      attributeProfileId: profile.id,
+      disabledProps: applyAttributeProfileToDisabledProps(cur.disabledProps, profile)
+    });
+  };
+
+  const handleQuickDeleteAttributeProfile = (profileId: string) => {
+    if (!profileId) return;
+    const latest = loadAttributeProfilesStore();
+    const nextStore = deleteAttributeProfileFromStore(latest, profileId);
+    if (!nextStore) return;
+    saveAttributeProfilesStore(nextStore);
+    setAttrStore(nextStore);
+    setNewGroupProfileId((prev) =>
+      prev === profileId || !nextStore.profiles.some((p) => p.id === prev)
+        ? nextStore.defaultProfileId
+        : prev
+    );
+    setMultiProfileId((prev) => (prev === profileId ? "" : prev));
+  };
+
+  const activeMultiIds = (state.multiIds || []).filter((id) => state.groups[String(id)]?.enabled);
+  const multiTargetGroups = activeMultiIds.map((id) => state.groups[String(id)]).filter((g): g is DynamicGroupItem => Boolean(g));
+
+  const updateMultipleGroups = (patch: Partial<DynamicGroupItem>) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (cur) nextGroups[String(id)] = { ...cur, ...patch };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const updateMultipleGroupsFluidScale = (patch: Partial<GroupFluidScaleConfig>) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        fluidScale: {
+          enabled: true,
+          ...(cur.fluidScale || {}),
+          ...patch
+        }
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const isMultiPropEnabled = (propKey: GroupStylePropKey): boolean => {
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return true;
+    return multiTargetGroups.some((g) => isPropEnabled(g, propKey));
+  };
+
+  const toggleMultipleGroupsProp = (propKey: GroupStylePropKey, nextEnabled: boolean) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      const nextDisabled: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(cur.disabledProps || {}) };
+      if (nextEnabled) {
+        delete nextDisabled[propKey];
+        if (propKey === "outerShadow") {
+          delete nextDisabled["shadow"];
+          delete nextDisabled["elevation"];
+        }
+      } else {
+        nextDisabled[propKey] = true;
+      }
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        disabledProps: Object.keys(nextDisabled).length > 0 ? nextDisabled : undefined
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const applyProfileToMultipleGroups = (profileId: string) => {
+    if (!profileId || !activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const profile = attrStore.profiles.find((p) => p.id === profileId);
+    if (!profile) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        attributeProfileId: profile.id,
+        disabledProps: applyAttributeProfileToDisabledProps(cur.disabledProps, profile)
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const isMultiStatePropEnabled = (stateKey: GroupStateKey, propKey: GroupStylePropKey): boolean => {
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return true;
+    return multiTargetGroups.some((g) => isStatePropEnabled(g, stateKey, propKey));
+  };
+
+  const toggleMultipleGroupsStateProp = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    propKey: GroupStylePropKey,
+    nextEnabled: boolean
+  ) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      const prevOv = cur.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+      const nextStDisabled: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(prevOv.disabledProps || {}) };
+      nextStDisabled[propKey] = !nextEnabled;
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        stateOverrides: {
+          ...(cur.stateOverrides || {}),
+          [stateKey]: { ...prevOv, disabledProps: nextStDisabled }
+        }
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const updateMultipleGroupsStateTheme = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    field: keyof GroupStateThemeValues,
+    val: string
+  ) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const themeBucket: "cl" | "ms" = isMs ? "ms" : "cl";
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      const prevOv = cur.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+      const updated: DynamicGroupItem = {
+        ...cur,
+        stateOverrides: {
+          ...(cur.stateOverrides || {}),
+          [stateKey]: {
+            ...prevOv,
+            [themeBucket]: { ...(prevOv[themeBucket] || {}), [field]: val }
+          }
+        }
+      };
+      if (stateKey === "hover" && field === "bg") {
+        if (isMs) updated.msHoverBg = val; else updated.clHoverBg = val;
+      } else if (stateKey === "hover" && field === "text") {
+        if (isMs) updated.msHoverText = val; else updated.clHoverText = val;
+      } else if (stateKey === "selected" && field === "bg") {
+        if (isMs) updated.msSelBg = val; else updated.clSelBg = val;
+      } else if (stateKey === "selected" && field === "text") {
+        if (isMs) updated.msSelText = val; else updated.clSelText = val;
+      } else if (stateKey === "focus" && field === "border") {
+        if (isMs) updated.msFocusBorder = val; else updated.clFocusBorder = val;
+      }
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = updated;
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const updateMultipleGroupsStateScalar = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    patch: Partial<Pick<GroupStateOverride, "borderMode" | "borderNone" | "borderWidth" | "borderStyle" | "fontWeight" | "radius" | "opacity" | "cursorNotAllowed">>
+  ) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      const prevOv = cur.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        stateOverrides: {
+          ...(cur.stateOverrides || {}),
+          [stateKey]: { ...prevOv, ...patch }
+        }
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const getSharedStateThemeVal = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    field: keyof GroupStateThemeValues
+  ): { val: string; mixed: boolean } => {
+    const themeBucket: "cl" | "ms" = isMs ? "ms" : "cl";
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return { val: "", mixed: false };
+    const first = String(multiTargetGroups[0]?.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]?.[themeBucket]?.[field] || "");
+    const allSame = multiTargetGroups.every(
+      (g) => String(g.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]?.[themeBucket]?.[field] || "") === first
+    );
+    return { val: first, mixed: !allSame };
+  };
+
+  const getSharedStateScalarVal = <K extends "borderMode" | "borderNone" | "borderWidth" | "borderStyle" | "fontWeight" | "radius" | "opacity" | "cursorNotAllowed">(
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    field: K
+  ): { val: GroupStateOverride[K]; mixed: boolean } => {
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return { val: undefined, mixed: false };
+    const first = multiTargetGroups[0]?.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]?.[field];
+    const allSame = multiTargetGroups.every(
+      (g) => (g.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]?.[field] ?? "") === (first ?? "")
+    );
+    return { val: first, mixed: !allSame };
+  };
+
+  const updateMultipleGroupsTextShadow = (nextCfg: ThemeTextShadowConfig) => {
+    updateMultipleGroups(isMs ? { msTextShadow: nextCfg } : { clTextShadow: nextCfg });
+  };
+
+  const updateMultipleGroupsStateTextShadow = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey,
+    nextCfg: ThemeTextShadowConfig
+  ) => {
+    if (!activeMultiIds.length) return;
+    const themeBucket: "cl" | "ms" = isMs ? "ms" : "cl";
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => {
+      const cur = nextGroups[String(id)];
+      if (!cur) return;
+      const prevOv = cur.stateOverrides?.[stateKey] || {};
+      nextGroups[String(id)] = {
+        ...cur,
+        stateOverrides: {
+          ...(cur.stateOverrides || {}),
+          [stateKey]: {
+            ...prevOv,
+            [themeBucket]: {
+              ...(prevOv[themeBucket] || {}),
+              textShadowEnabled: nextCfg.textShadowEnabled,
+              textShadowX: nextCfg.textShadowX,
+              textShadowY: nextCfg.textShadowY,
+              textShadowBlur: nextCfg.textShadowBlur,
+              textShadowColor: nextCfg.textShadowColor,
+              textShadowOpacity: nextCfg.textShadowOpacity,
+              textShadowCustom: nextCfg.textShadowCustom
+            }
+          }
+        }
+      };
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const getSharedDefaultTextShadow = (): { val: ThemeTextShadowConfig | undefined; mixed: boolean } => {
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return { val: undefined, mixed: false };
+    const first = resolveSingleDefaultTextShadow(multiTargetGroups[0]!);
+    const sig = JSON.stringify(first || {});
+    const allSame = multiTargetGroups.every((g) => JSON.stringify(resolveSingleDefaultTextShadow(g) || {}) === sig);
+    return { val: first, mixed: !allSame };
+  };
+
+  const getSharedStateTextShadow = (
+    stateKey: SubGroupStateKey
+  ): { val: ThemeTextShadowConfig | undefined; mixed: boolean } => {
+    if (!multiTargetGroups.length) return { val: undefined, mixed: false };
+    const first = resolveSingleStateTextShadow(multiTargetGroups[0]?.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]);
+    const sig = JSON.stringify(first || {});
+    const allSame = multiTargetGroups.every(
+      (g) => JSON.stringify(resolveSingleStateTextShadow(g.stateOverrides?.[stateKey]) || {}) === sig
+    );
+    return { val: first, mixed: !allSame };
+  };
+
+  const toggleMultiId = (id: number) => {
+    const curSet = new Set(activeMultiIds);
+    if (curSet.has(id)) curSet.delete(id);
+    else curSet.add(id);
+    commitState({ ...state, multiIds: Array.from(curSet).sort((a, b) => a - b) });
+  };
+
+  const getSharedProp = <K extends keyof DynamicGroupItem>(key: K): { val: DynamicGroupItem[K]; mixed: boolean } => {
+    const firstGroup = multiTargetGroups[0];
+    if (!firstGroup) return { val: "" as any, mixed: false };
+    const first = firstGroup[key];
+    const allSame = multiTargetGroups.every((g) => g[key] === first);
+    return { val: first, mixed: !allSame };
+  };
+
+  const removeGroup = (id: number) => {
+    const cur = state.groups[String(id)];
+    if (!cur) return;
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups, [String(id)]: { ...cur, enabled: false } };
+    const remaining = Object.values(nextGroups).filter((g) => g && g.enabled).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
+    commitState({ ...state, selectedId: remaining[0]?.id || 1, groups: nextGroups });
+  };
+
+  const executeConfirmedDelete = () => {
+    if (confirmDeleteId === null) return;
+    if (confirmDeleteId === "revert") {
+      setConfirmDeleteId(null);
+      handleDropdownChange("__APPLY_REVERT_NOW__");
+      return;
+    }
+    const nextGroups = { ...state.groups };
+    if (confirmDeleteId === "multi") {
+      activeMultiIds.forEach((id) => { delete nextGroups[String(id)]; });
+    } else {
+      delete nextGroups[String(confirmDeleteId)];
+    }
+    const remaining = Object.values(nextGroups).filter((g): g is DynamicGroupItem => Boolean(g && g.enabled)).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
+    const nextSel = remaining[0]?.id ?? 0;
+    commitState({ selectedId: nextSel, multiIds: remaining.map((g) => g.id), groups: nextGroups, imageGroups: state.imageGroups, designTokens: state.designTokens });
+    setConfirmDeleteId(null);
+    setMultiPickerOpen(false);
+  };
+  const isMultiMode = state.selectedId === "multi";
+  const singleGroup = (!isMultiMode && enabledGroups.length > 0) ? (state.groups[String(state.selectedId)] || enabledGroups[0] || null) : null;
+  const activeTitle = enabledGroups.length === 0 ? "No Groups (0)" : isMultiMode ? ("Multiple (" + activeMultiIds.length + ")") : (singleGroup?.name || ("Group " + state.selectedId));
+
+  const portalTarget = triggerRef.current?.ownerDocument?.body || (typeof document !== "undefined" ? document.body : null);
+
+  // Live Canvas State Preview: projects active Hover / Focus / Selected / Sel+Hover / Disabled styles
+  // directly onto matching canvas elements in real time via transient `#ecmd-theme-live-state-preview`
+  // with ZERO mutation of persisted theme data, and cleans up immediately on Default / tab switch / close / unmount.
+  useLayoutEffect(() => {
+    const ownerDocs = [
+      triggerRef.current?.ownerDocument,
+      modalRef.current?.ownerDocument
+    ];
+    if (!panelOpen || activeStudioTab !== "groups" || !liveStatePreviewEnabled || activeState === "default") {
+      setLiveStatePreview(null);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (isMultiMode) {
+      const activeTargets = multiTargetGroups.filter((g) => g && g.enabled);
+      if (activeTargets.length === 0) {
+        setLiveStatePreview(null);
+        return;
+      }
+      setLiveStatePreview({
+        groupId: activeTargets.map((g) => String(g.id)).join(","),
+        selector: activeTargets.map((g) => g.selector || (".gm-group-" + g.id)).join(", "),
+        stateKey: activeState,
+        groups: activeTargets,
+        activeTheme: editPalette,
+        designTokens,
+        targetDocuments: ownerDocs
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+    if (!singleGroup || !singleGroup.enabled) {
+      setLiveStatePreview(null);
+      return;
+    }
+    setLiveStatePreview({
+      groupId: String(singleGroup.id),
+      selector: singleGroup.selector || (".gm-group-" + singleGroup.id),
+      stateKey: activeState,
+      group: singleGroup,
+      activeTheme: editPalette,
+      designTokens,
+      targetDocuments: ownerDocs
+    });
+  }, [
+    panelOpen,
+    activeStudioTab,
+    liveStatePreviewEnabled,
+    activeState,
+    isMultiMode,
+    singleGroup,
+    multiTargetGroups,
+    editPalette,
+    designTokens
+  ]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    return () => {
+      setLiveStatePreview(null);
+      if (copyUndoTimerRef.current) {
+        clearTimeout(copyUndoTimerRef.current);
+      }
+    };
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!panelOpen || activeStudioTab !== "groups") {
+      setCopyMenuTargetId(null);
+      setCopySearch("");
+    }
+  }, [panelOpen, activeStudioTab]);
+
+  const activeStateShortLabel =
+    GROUP_STATE_TABS.find((t) => t.id === activeState)?.shortLabel || activeState;
+
+  const handleCopyGroupAttributesFrom = (
+    targetGroupId: number,
+    sourceGroup: DynamicGroupItem
+  ) => {
+    const prevTarget = state.groups[String(targetGroupId)];
+    if (!prevTarget || sourceGroup.id === targetGroupId) return;
+    const preCopySnapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevTarget)) as DynamicGroupItem;
+    const nextGroups = copyThemeGroupAttributes({
+      themes: state.groups,
+      activeThemeId: editPalette,
+      sourceGroupId: sourceGroup.id,
+      targetGroupId,
+      scope: copyScope,
+      stateKey: activeState
+    });
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: nextGroups });
+    setCopyMenuTargetId(null);
+    setCopySearch("");
+    const scopeLabel =
+      copyScope === "allThemesAllStates"
+        ? "All Themes"
+        : copyScope === "activeThemeCurrentState"
+          ? activeStateShortLabel
+          : "All States";
+    if (copyUndoTimerRef.current) {
+      clearTimeout(copyUndoTimerRef.current);
+    }
+    setCopyUndoNotice({
+      targetId: targetGroupId,
+      sourceName: sourceGroup.name || ("Group " + sourceGroup.id),
+      scopeLabel,
+      previousGroup: preCopySnapshot
+    });
+    copyUndoTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
+      setCopyUndoNotice((prev) => (prev && prev.targetId === targetGroupId ? null : prev));
+    }, 10000);
+  };
+
+  const handleUndoCopyGroupAttributes = () => {
+    if (!copyUndoNotice) return;
+    if (copyUndoTimerRef.current) {
+      clearTimeout(copyUndoTimerRef.current);
+      copyUndoTimerRef.current = null;
+    }
+    const { targetId, previousGroup } = copyUndoNotice;
+    const restoredGroups = {
+      ...state.groups,
+      [String(targetId)]: previousGroup
+    };
+    commitState({ ...state, groups: restoredGroups });
+    setCopyUndoNotice(null);
+  };
+
+  const renderCopyAttributesTrigger = (targetGroup: DynamicGroupItem) => {
+    const isOpen = copyMenuTargetId === targetGroup.id;
+    const otherCount = enabledGroups.filter((g) => g.id !== targetGroup.id).length;
+    return (
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        data-ecmd-copy-attrs-trigger={targetGroup.id}
+        aria-expanded={isOpen}
+        aria-haspopup="true"
+        disabled={otherCount === 0}
+        onClick={(e) => {
+          e.stopPropagation();
+          if (otherCount === 0) return;
+          setCopySearch("");
+          setCopyMenuTargetId((prev) => (prev === targetGroup.id ? null : targetGroup.id));
+        }}
+        title={
+          otherCount === 0
+            ? "Create at least one other group to copy attributes from"
+            : "Copy visual style attributes from another group while keeping this group's name and CSS selector"
+        }
+        style={{
+          padding: "2px 7px",
+          fontSize: "10px",
+          fontWeight: 700,
+          borderRadius: "5px",
+          border: isOpen ? "1px solid #0D9488" : "1px solid #CBD5E1",
+          background: isOpen ? "rgba(13, 148, 136, 0.12)" : "#F8FAFC",
+          color: otherCount === 0 ? "#94A3B8" : isOpen ? "#0F766E" : "#334155",
+          cursor: otherCount === 0 ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
+          whiteSpace: "nowrap",
+          flexShrink: 0,
+          opacity: otherCount === 0 ? 0.6 : 1
+        }}
+      >
+        Copy Attributes From {isOpen ? "Γû┤" : "Γû╛"}
+      </button>
+    );
+  };
+
+  const renderCopyAttributesDropdownAndNotice = (targetGroup: DynamicGroupItem) => {
+    const isOpen = copyMenuTargetId === targetGroup.id;
+    const showUndo = copyUndoNotice && copyUndoNotice.targetId === targetGroup.id;
+    if (!isOpen && !showUndo) return null;
+
+    const sourceCandidates = isOpen
+      ? filterAndSortThemeGroups(
+          enabledGroups.filter((g) => g.id !== targetGroup.id),
+          {
+            query: copySearch,
+            sortBy: "az",
+            tokensMap: designTokens
+          }
+        )
+      : [];
+
+    return (
+      <>
+        {isOpen && (
+          <div
+            data-ecmd-copy-attrs-menu={targetGroup.id}
+            onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
+            style={{
+              border: "1px solid #CBD5E1",
+              borderRadius: "8px",
+              background: "#FFFFFF",
+              boxShadow: "0 10px 24px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.14)",
+              padding: "7px 8px",
+              display: "flex",
+              flexDirection: "column",
+              gap: "6px"
+            }}
+          >
+            {/* Scope Pills: All States (default) | This State (<ActiveState>) | All Themes */}
+            <div
+              data-ecmd-copy-attrs-scopes="true"
+              style={{
+                display: "grid",
+                gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))",
+                gap: "3px",
+                background: "#F1F5F9",
+                padding: "2px",
+                borderRadius: "6px",
+                border: "1px solid #E2E8F0"
+              }}
+            >
+              {(
+                [
+                  { id: "activeThemeAllStates", label: "All States", title: "Copy all 6 states for the active theme" },
+                  { id: "activeThemeCurrentState", label: `This State (${activeStateShortLabel})`, title: `Copy only ${activeStateShortLabel} state for the active theme` },
+                  { id: "allThemesAllStates", label: "All Themes", title: "Copy all 6 states across every theme (Light & Dark)" }
+                ] as const
+              ).map((opt) => {
+                const active = copyScope === opt.id;
+                return (
+                  <button
+                    key={opt.id}
+                    type="button"
+                    data-ecmd-copy-scope-pill={opt.id}
+                    aria-pressed={active}
+                    title={opt.title}
+                    onClick={() => setCopyScope(opt.id)}
+                    style={{
+                      padding: "3px 4px",
+                      fontSize: "9.5px",
+                      fontWeight: 700,
+                      borderRadius: "4px",
+                      border: "none",
+                      background: active ? "#FFFFFF" : "transparent",
+                      color: active ? "#0F766E" : "#475569",
+                      boxShadow: active ? "0 1px 2px rgba(15,23,42,0.10)" : "none",
+                      cursor: "pointer",
+                      overflow: "hidden",
+                      textOverflow: "ellipsis",
+                      whiteSpace: "nowrap"
+                    }}
+                  >
+                    {opt.label}
+                  </button>
+                );
+              })}
+            </div>
+
+            {/* Quick-Filter Search Input */}
+            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px" }}>
+              <input
+                type="text"
+                aria-label="Filter source groups"
+                data-ecmd-copy-attrs-search={targetGroup.id}
+                value={copySearch}
+                placeholder="Filter source groups..."
+                onChange={(e) => setCopySearch(e.target.value)}
+                onKeyDown={(e) => {
+                  e.stopPropagation();
+                  if (e.key === "Escape") {
+                    setCopyMenuTargetId(null);
+                    setCopySearch("");
+                  }
+                }}
+                style={{
+                  flex: 1,
+                  minWidth: 0,
+                  padding: "4px 7px",
+                  fontSize: "10.5px",
+                  borderRadius: "6px",
+                  border: "1px solid #CBD5E1",
+                  background: "#F8FAFC",
+                  color: "#0F172A",
+                  outline: "none"
+                }}
+              />
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                aria-label="Close Copy Attributes menu"
+                onClick={() => {
+                  setCopyMenuTargetId(null);
+                  setCopySearch("");
+                }}
+                style={{
+                  border: "1px solid #E2E8F0",

+function loadCustomGradients(): PresetColorToken[] {
+  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
+  try {
+    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(CUSTOM_GRADIENTS_KEY);

+    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);

+  } catch {
+    return [];
+  }

+  { id: "recessed-input", label: "Recessed Input", value: "inset 0px 2px 4px 0px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)" },
+  { id: "pressed-button", label: "Pressed Button", value: "inset 0px 3px 6px 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)" },
+  { id: "subtle-inner-rim", label: "Subtle Inner Rim", value: "inset 0px 0px 0px 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.14)" },
+  { id: "inner-glow", label: "Inner Glow", value: "inset 0px 0px 12px 1px rgba(20,184,166,0.35)" }
+];
+
+export interface ParsedShadowFields {
+  x: number;
+  y: number;
+  blur: number;
+  spread: number;
+  colorHex: string;
+  opacity: number;
+}
+
+export function splitShadowLayers(raw?: string): string[] {
+  const s = (raw || "").trim();
+  if (!s) return [];
+  const layers: string[] = [];
+  let cur = "";
+  let depth = 0;
+  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
+    const ch = s[i];
+    if (ch === "(") depth++;
+    else if (ch === ")") depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
+    if (ch === "," && depth === 0) {
+      if (cur.trim()) layers.push(cur.trim());
+      cur = "";
+    } else {
+      cur += ch;
+    }
+  }
+  if (cur.trim()) layers.push(cur.trim());
+  return layers;
+}
+
+export function normalizeOuterShadow(raw?: string): string {
+  const s = (raw || "").trim();
+  if (!s || /^unset$/i.test(s)) return "";
+  return s;
+}
+
+export function ensureInsetShadow(raw?: string): string {
+  const s = (raw || "").trim();
+  if (!s || /^(unset|none)$/i.test(s)) return "";
+  const layers = splitShadowLayers(s);
+  if (!layers.length) return "";
+  return layers
+    .map((layer) => (/\binset\b/i.test(layer) ? layer : "inset " + layer))
+    .join(", ");
+}
+
+export function resolveCombinedBoxShadow(opts: {
+  outerShadow?: string | undefined;
+  innerShadow?: string | undefined;
+  legacyShadow?: string | undefined;
+  legacyElevation?: string | undefined;
+  disabledProps?: Record<string, boolean> | undefined;
+}): string {
+  const disabled = opts.disabledProps;
+  const outerOff = Boolean(disabled?.["outerShadow"] || disabled?.["shadow"] || disabled?.["elevation"]);
+  const innerOff = Boolean(disabled?.["innerShadow"]);
+
+  const rawOuter = outerOff
+    ? ""
+    : opts.outerShadow !== undefined
+      ? opts.outerShadow
+      : opts.legacyElevation !== undefined
+        ? opts.legacyElevation
+        : (opts.legacyShadow ?? "");
+
+  const outerClean = normalizeOuterShadow(rawOuter);
+  const innerClean = innerOff ? "" : ensureInsetShadow(opts.innerShadow);
+
+  if (outerClean && outerClean.toLowerCase() !== "none" && innerClean) {
+    return outerClean + ", " + innerClean;
+  }
+  if (innerClean) return innerClean;
+  if (outerClean) return outerClean;
+  return "";
+}
+
+function hexToRgbTuple(hex: string): { r: number; g: number; b: number } {
+  const clean = (hex || "").trim().replace(/^#/, "");
+  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(clean)) {
+    return {
+      r: parseInt(clean.charAt(0) + clean.charAt(0), 16),
+      g: parseInt(clean.charAt(1) + clean.charAt(1), 16),
+      b: parseInt(clean.charAt(2) + clean.charAt(2), 16)
+    };
+  }
+  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(clean)) {
+    return {
+      r: parseInt(clean.slice(0, 2), 16),
+      g: parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16),
+      b: parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16)
+    };
+  }
+  return { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 };
+}
+
+export function parseShadowCssToFields(raw: string, isInner: boolean): ParsedShadowFields {
+  const fallback: ParsedShadowFields = {
+    x: 0,
+    y: isInner ? 2 : 4,
+    blur: isInner ? 4 : 12,
+    spread: 0,
+    colorHex: "#000000",
+    opacity: isInner ? 25 : 20
+  };
+  const s = (raw || "").trim();
+  if (!s || /^(unset|none)$/i.test(s)) return fallback;
+
+  const firstLayer = splitShadowLayers(s)[0] || s;
+  let rest = firstLayer.replace(/\binset\b/gi, "").trim();
+
+  let colorHex = fallback.colorHex;
+  let opacity = fallback.opacity;
+
+  const rgbaMatch = /rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)(?:\s*,\s*([0-9.]+))?\s*\)/i.exec(rest);
+  if (rgbaMatch && rgbaMatch[1] && rgbaMatch[2] && rgbaMatch[3]) {
+    const r = parseInt(rgbaMatch[1], 10);
+    const g = parseInt(rgbaMatch[2], 10);
+    const b = parseInt(rgbaMatch[3], 10);
+    const a = rgbaMatch[4] !== undefined ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, parseFloat(rgbaMatch[4]))) : 1;
+    colorHex = "#" + toHex2(r) + toHex2(g) + toHex2(b);
+    opacity = Math.round(a * 100);
+    rest = rest.replace(rgbaMatch[0], " ").trim();
+  } else {
+    const hexMatch = /#([0-9a-fA-F]{3}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{8})\b/.exec(rest);
+    if (hexMatch && hexMatch[1]) {
+      const h = hexMatch[1];
+      if (h.length === 3) {
+        colorHex = "#" + (h.charAt(0) + h.charAt(0) + h.charAt(1) + h.charAt(1) + h.charAt(2) + h.charAt(2)).toUpperCase();
+        opacity = 100;
+      } else if (h.length === 6) {
+        colorHex = "#" + h.toUpperCase();
+        opacity = 100;
+      } else if (h.length === 8) {
+        colorHex = "#" + h.slice(0, 6).toUpperCase();
+        opacity = Math.round((parseInt(h.slice(6, 8), 16) / 255) * 100);
+      }
+      rest = rest.replace(hexMatch[0], " ").trim();
+    } else if (/\bwhite\b/i.test(rest)) {
+      colorHex = "#FFFFFF";
+      opacity = 100;
+      rest = rest.replace(/\bwhite\b/i, " ").trim();
+    } else if (/\bblack\b/i.test(rest)) {
+      colorHex = "#000000";
+      opacity = 100;
+      rest = rest.replace(/\bblack\b/i, " ").trim();
+    }
+  }
+
+  const numTokens = rest.match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:px)?/g) || [];
+  const nums = numTokens.map((t) => Math.round(parseFloat(t)));
+  return {
+    x: nums[0] ?? fallback.x,
+    y: nums[1] ?? fallback.y,
+    blur: Math.max(0, nums[2] ?? fallback.blur),
+    spread: nums[3] ?? 0,
+    colorHex,
+    opacity
+  };
+}
+
+export function formatShadowFieldsToCss(fields: ParsedShadowFields, isInner: boolean): string {
+  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgbTuple(fields.colorHex);
+  const clampedOpacity = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(fields.opacity)));
+  const alphaStr = clampedOpacity >= 100 ? "1" : clampedOpacity <= 0 ? "0" : (clampedOpacity / 100).toFixed(2);
+  const prefix = isInner ? "inset " : "";
+  return `${prefix}${Math.round(fields.x)}px ${Math.round(fields.y)}px ${Math.max(0, Math.round(fields.blur))}px ${Math.round(fields.spread)}px rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alphaStr})`;
+}
+
+export interface EcmdPlasmicShadowRowProps {
+  mode: "outer" | "inner";
+  propKey: "outerShadow" | "innerShadow";
+  label: string;
+  value: string;
+  isMixed?: boolean | undefined;
+  enabled?: boolean | undefined;
+  onToggleEnabled?: ((nextEnabled: boolean) => void) | undefined;
+  onChange: (val: string) => void;
+  tokenBinding?: EcmdTokenBindingContext | undefined;
+}
+
+export function EcmdPlasmicShadowRow({
+  mode,
+  propKey,
+  label,
+  value,
+  isMixed,
+  enabled = true,
+  onToggleEnabled,
+  onChange,
+  tokenBinding
+}: EcmdPlasmicShadowRowProps) {
+  const isInner = mode === "inner";
+  const presets = isInner ? INNER_SHADOW_PRESETS : OUTER_SHADOW_PRESETS;
+  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
+  const [editTokenMode, setEditTokenMode] = useState(false);
+  const [editTokenScope, setEditTokenScope] = useState<"all" | "theme">("all");
+
+  const linkedToken = tokenBinding
+    ? findDesignTokenById(tokenBinding.tokens, tokenBinding.linkedTokenId)
+    : undefined;
+
+  const normVal = (value || "").trim();
+  const isRowEnabled = enabled !== false;
+  const fields = parseShadowCssToFields(normVal, isInner);
+  const { angle: shadowAngle, distance: shadowDistance } = shadowOffsetsToAngleDistance(fields.x, fields.y);
+
+  const emitShadowValue = (nextVal: string) => {
+    if (linkedToken && editTokenMode && tokenBinding?.onEditLinkedToken) {
+      tokenBinding.onEditLinkedToken(
+        linkedToken.id,
+        {
+          css: nextVal,
+          inset: isInner || /\binset\b/i.test(nextVal),
+          layers: [
+            {
+              ...parseShadowCssToFields(nextVal, isInner),
+              color: parseShadowCssToFields(nextVal, isInner).colorHex,
+              inset: isInner || /\binset\b/i.test(nextVal),
+            },
+          ],
+        },
+        editTokenScope
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+    onChange(nextVal);
+  };
+
+  const matchedPreset = !isMixed
+    ? presets.find((p) => p.value.toLowerCase() === (normVal.toLowerCase() === "unset" ? "" : normVal.toLowerCase()))
+    : undefined;
+  const selectValue = isMixed ? "__MIXED__" : (matchedPreset ? matchedPreset.id : "__CUSTOM__");
+
+  const handlePresetChange = (presetId: string) => {
+    if (presetId === "__MIXED__") return;
+    if (presetId === "__CUSTOM__") {
+      setExpanded(true);
+      if (!normVal || /^(unset|none)$/i.test(normVal)) {
+        emitShadowValue(formatShadowFieldsToCss(fields, isInner));
+      }
+      return;
+    }
+    const found = presets.find((p) => p.id === presetId);
+    if (found) {
+      emitShadowValue(found.value);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const updateField = (patch: Partial<ParsedShadowFields>) => {
+    const next: ParsedShadowFields = { ...fields, ...patch };
+    emitShadowValue(formatShadowFieldsToCss(next, isInner));
+  };
+
+  const handleRawChange = (rawInput: string) => {
+    const trimmed = rawInput.trim();
+    if (!trimmed) {
+      emitShadowValue("");
+      return;
+    }
+    if (isInner) {
+      // Preserve inset automatically unless user is mid-typing the keyword "inset"
+      if (/^i(n(s(e(t)?)?)?)?$/i.test(trimmed)) {
+        emitShadowValue(rawInput);
+      } else {
+        emitShadowValue(ensureInsetShadow(rawInput));
+      }
+    } else {
+      emitShadowValue(rawInput);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const safeColorHex = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(fields.colorHex) ? fields.colorHex : "#000000";
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-ecmd-prop-row={propKey}
+      data-ecmd-prop-enabled={isRowEnabled ? "true" : "false"}
+      style={{
+        display: "flex",
+        flexDirection: "column",
+        gap: "5px",
+        padding: "4px 0",
+        opacity: isRowEnabled ? 1 : 0.45,
+        transition: "opacity 0.15s ease"
+      }}
+    >
+      {/* Compact Header Row: ON/OFF Checkbox + Collapsible Label + 1-Click Preset Selector */}
+      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "6px" }}>
+        {onToggleEnabled && (
+          <input
+            type="checkbox"
+            checked={isRowEnabled}
+            aria-label={`Enable ${label}`}
+            data-ecmd-prop-toggle={propKey}
+            title={isRowEnabled ? `Turn OFF ${label} (preserves saved values, lets Plasmic Design tab control box-shadow)` : `Turn ON ${label} (restores saved values)`}
+            onChange={(e) => onToggleEnabled(e.target.checked)}
+            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
+            style={{ width: "13px", height: "13px", margin: 0, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0, accentColor: "#0D9488" }}
+          />
+        )}
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          data-ecmd-shadow-expand={propKey}
+          aria-expanded={expanded}
+          onClick={() => setExpanded((v) => !v)}
+          title={expanded ? `Collapse ${label} builder` : `Expand ${label} visual controls`}
+          style={{
+            display: "flex",
+            alignItems: "center",
+            gap: "3px",
+            width: onToggleEnabled ? "86px" : "98px",
+            flexShrink: 0,
+            padding: "2px 0",
+            border: "none",
+            background: "transparent",
+            color: "#334155",
+            fontSize: "11.5px",
+            fontWeight: 500,
+            cursor: "pointer",
+            textAlign: "left"
+          }}
+        >
+          <span style={{ fontSize: "9px", color: "#64748B", width: "9px", flexShrink: 0 }}>{expanded ? "Γû╛" : "Γû╕"}</span>
+          <span style={{ overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{label}</span>
+        </button>
+
+        <select
+          aria-label={`${label} Preset`}
+          data-ecmd-shadow-preset={propKey}
+          value={selectValue}
+          onChange={(e) => handlePresetChange(e.target.value)}
+          style={{
+            flex: 1,
+            minWidth: 0,
+            padding: "4px 6px",
+            fontSize: "11px",
+            fontWeight: 500,
+            borderRadius: "6px",
+            border: "1px solid #E2E8F0",
+            background: "#F8FAFC",
+            color: "#0F172A",
+            cursor: "pointer",
+            outline: "none"
+          }}
+        >
+          {isMixed && <option value="__MIXED__">Mixed ΓÇö select preset</option>}
+          {presets.map((p) => (
+            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
+              {p.label}
+            </option>
+          ))}
+          <option value="__CUSTOM__">Custom</option>
+        </select>
+
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          onClick={() => setExpanded((v) => !v)}
+          title={expanded ? "Hide shadow controls" : "Fine-tune X, Y, Blur, Spread, Color & Opacity"}
+          style={{
+            width: "22px",
+            height: "24px",
+            borderRadius: "5px",
+            border: expanded ? "1px solid #0D9488" : "1px solid #E2E8F0",
+            background: expanded ? "rgba(13, 148, 136, 0.10)" : "#F8FAFC",
+            color: expanded ? "#0F766E" : "#475569",
+            fontSize: "11px",
+            fontWeight: 700,
+            cursor: "pointer",
+            flexShrink: 0,
+            padding: 0,
+            display: "flex",
+            alignItems: "center",
+            justifyContent: "center"
+          }}
+        >
+          ΓÜÖ
+        </button>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Linked Token Pill right on Groups Inspector Row */}
+      {linkedToken && (
+        <div style={{ marginLeft: "18px", display: "flex", alignItems: "center" }}>
+          <EcmdLinkedTokenPill
+            token={linkedToken}
+            attrTag={propKey}
+            onClickPill={() => setExpanded(true)}
+            onDetach={() => {
+              setEditTokenMode(false);
+              tokenBinding?.onDetachToken();
+            }}
+          />
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {/* Collapsible Visual Shadow Builder */}
+      {expanded && (
+        <div
+          data-ecmd-shadow-panel={propKey}
+          style={{
+            marginLeft: "18px",
+            padding: "8px 9px",
+            borderRadius: "8px",
+            border: "1px solid #E2E8F0",
+            background: "#F8FAFC",
+            display: "flex",
+            flexDirection: "column",
+            gap: "7px"
+          }}
+        >
+          {/* Reusable Box Shadow Tokens Bar */}
+          {tokenBinding && (
+            <EcmdTokenPopoverSection
+              tokens={tokenBinding.tokens}
+              compatibleTypes={["boxShadow"]}
+              activePalette={tokenBinding.activePalette}
+              linkedTokenId={tokenBinding.linkedTokenId}
+              editTokenMode={editTokenMode}
+              onToggleEditTokenMode={setEditTokenMode}
+              editTokenScope={editTokenScope}
+              onChangeEditTokenScope={setEditTokenScope}
+              onSelectToken={(tokId) => tokenBinding.onSelectToken(tokId)}
+              onDetachToken={() => {
+                setEditTokenMode(false);
+                tokenBinding.onDetachToken();
+              }}
+              onSaveAsToken={(name) => tokenBinding.onSaveAsToken(name)}
+              saveButtonLabel="+ Save Current Shadow as Token"
+              defaultTokenName={isInner ? "Recessed Inner Shadow" : "Glass Card Shadow"}
+              attrTag={propKey}
+            />
+          )}
+
+          {/* Angle & Distance Ergonomic Polar Controls */}
+          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "6px 8px" }}>
+            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "2px" }}>
+              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "4px" }}>
+                <span style={{ fontSize: "10px", fontWeight: 600, color: "#475569" }}>Angle (┬░)</span>
+                <input
+                  type="number"
+                  aria-label={`${label} Angle`}
+                  data-ecmd-shadow-input={`${propKey}-angle`}
+                  min={0}
+                  max={360}
+                  value={shadowAngle}
+                  onChange={(e) => {
+                    const deg = Number(e.target.value);
+                    if (!Number.isNaN(deg)) {
+                      const nextOffsets = shadowAngleDistanceToOffsets(deg, Math.max(1, shadowDistance));
+                      updateField(nextOffsets);
+                    }
+                  }}
+                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
+                  style={{ width: "46px", padding: "2px 4px", fontSize: "10.5px", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", textAlign: "right", borderRadius: "4px", border: "1px solid #CBD5E1", background: "#FFFFFF", color: "#0F172A", outline: "none" }}
+                />
+              </div>
+              <input
+                type="range"
+                aria-label={`${label} Angle Slider`}
+                min={0}
+                max={360}
+                value={shadowAngle}
+                onChange={(e) => {
+                  const nextOffsets = shadowAngleDistanceToOffsets(Number(e.target.value), Math.max(1, shadowDistance));
+                  updateField(nextOffsets);
+                }}
+                style={{ width: "100%", height: "4px", accentColor: "#0D9488", cursor: "pointer" }}
+              />
+            </div>
+
+            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "2px" }}>
+              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "4px" }}>
+                <span style={{ fontSize: "10px", fontWeight: 600, color: "#475569" }}>Distance (px)</span>
+                <input
+                  type="number"
+                  aria-label={`${label} Distance`}
+                  data-ecmd-shadow-input={`${propKey}-distance`}
+                  min={0}
+                  max={60}
+                  value={shadowDistance}
+                  onChange={(e) => {
+                    const dist = Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) || 0);
+                    const nextOffsets = shadowAngleDistanceToOffsets(shadowAngle, dist);
+                    updateField(nextOffsets);
+                  }}
+                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
+                  style={{ width: "46px", padding: "2px 4px", fontSize: "10.5px", fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace", textAlign: "right", borderRadius: "4px", border: "1px solid #CBD5E1", background: "#FFFFFF", color: "#0F172A", outline: "none" }}
+                />
+              </div>
+              <input
+                type="range"
+                aria-label={`${label} Distance Slider`}
+                min={0}
+                max={60}
+                value={Math.min(60, shadowDistance)}
+                onChange={(e) => {
+                  const nextOffsets = shadowAngleDistanceToOffsets(shadowAngle, Number(e.target.value));
+                  updateField(nextOffsets);
+                }}
+                style={{ width: "100%", height: "4px", accentColor: "#0D9488", cursor: "pointer" }}
+              />
+            </div>
+          </div>
+
+          {/* Compact 2x2 X / Y / Blur / Spread Numeric + Slider Controls */}
+          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "6px 8px" }}>
+            {(
+              [
+                { key: "x", label: "X (px)", min: -50, max: 50, val: fields.x },
+                { key: "y", label: "Y (px)", min: -50, max: 50, val: fields.y },
+                { key: "blur", label: "Blur (px)", min: 0, max: 80, val: fields.blur },
+                { key: "spread", label: "Spread (px)", min: -30, max: 40, val: fields.spread }
+              ] as const
+            ).map((ctrl) => (
+              <div key={ctrl.key} style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "2px" }}>
+                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: "4px" }}>
+                  <span style={{ fontSize: "10px", fontWeight: 600, color: "#475569" }}>{ctrl.label}</span>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    aria-label={`${label} ${ctrl.label}`}
+                    data-ecmd-shadow-input={`${propKey}-${ctrl.key}`}
+                    min={ctrl.min}
+                    max={ctrl.max}
+                    value={ctrl.val}
+                    onChange={(e) => {
+                      const num = Number(e.target.value);
+                      if (!Number.isNaN(num)) updateField({ [ctrl.key]: num });
+                    }}
+                    onKeyDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
+                    style={{
+                      width: "46px",
+                      padding: "2px 4px",
+                      fontSize: "10.5px",
+                      fontFamily: "ui-monospace, monospace",
+                      textAlign: "right",
+                      borderRadius: "4px",
+                      border: "1px solid #CBD5E1",
+                      background: "#FFFFFF",
+                      color: "#0F172A",
+                      outline: "none"
+                    }}
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <input
+                  type="range"
+                  aria-label={`${label} ${ctrl.label} Slider`}
+                  min={ctrl.min}
+                  max={ctrl.max}
+                  value={Math.max(ctrl.min, Math.min(ctrl.max, ctrl.val))}
+                  onChange={(e) => updateField({ [ctrl.key]: Number(e.target.value) })}
+                  style={{ width: "100%", height: "4px", accentColor: "#0D9488", cursor: "pointer" }}
+                />
+              </div>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+
+          {/* Color (#HEX Picker + Text Input) & Opacity (%) Controls */}
+          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "6px 8px", alignItems: "end" }}>
+            <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "2px" }}>
+              <span style={{ fontSize: "10px", fontWeight: 600, color: "#475569" }}>Color (#HEX)</span>
+              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", padding: "2px 4px", borderRadius: "5px", border: "1px solid #CBD5E1", background: "#FFFFFF" }}>
+                <input
+                  type="color"
+                  aria-label={`${label} Color Picker`}
+                  value={safeColorHex}
+                  onChange={(e) => updateField({ colorHex: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })}

+function parseOpacityCss(raw?: string): string {
+  const s = (raw || "").trim();
+  if (!s) return "";
+  if (s.endsWith("%")) {
+    const n = parseFloat(s.slice(0, -1));
+    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "";
+    return String(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.round(n) / 100)));
+  }
+  const n = parseFloat(s);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "";
+  if (n > 1) return String(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.round(n) / 100)));
+  return String(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n)));
+}
+
+function parseFiniteNonNegPx(raw?: string | number | null): number | null {
+  if (raw === undefined || raw === null) return null;
+  const str = String(raw).trim().replace(/px$/i, "");
+  if (!str) return null;
+  const n = Number(str);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) return null;
+  return n;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Generates a smooth CSS clamp() expression linearly interpolated between
+ * 375px Mobile and 1440px Desktop viewports.
+ */
+export function buildFluidClampCss(
+  mobilePxRaw?: string | number | null,
+  desktopPxRaw?: string | number | null,
+  minVw = 375,
+  maxVw = 1440
+): string {
+  const mob = parseFiniteNonNegPx(mobilePxRaw);
+  const desk = parseFiniteNonNegPx(desktopPxRaw);
+  if (mob === null && desk === null) return "";
+  if (mob === null && desk !== null) return `${desk}px`;
+  if (mob !== null && desk === null) return `${mob}px`;
+  const m = mob as number;
+  const d = desk as number;
+  if (m === d) return `${d}px`;
+
+  const minVal = Math.min(m, d);
+  const maxVal = Math.max(m, d);
+  const span = Math.max(1, maxVw - minVw);
+  const slopeVw = Number((((d - m) / span) * 100).toFixed(4));
+  const interceptPx = Number((m - (d - m) * (minVw / span)).toFixed(2));
+  const preferred =
+    interceptPx >= 0
+      ? `calc(${interceptPx}px + ${slopeVw}vw)`
+      : `calc(${slopeVw}vw - ${Math.abs(interceptPx)}px)`;
+  return `clamp(${minVal}px, ${preferred}, ${maxVal}px)`;
+}
+
+export function wrapCssWithBreakpointMedia(
+  css: string,
+  breakpointTarget?: ThemeGroupBreakpointTarget
+): string {
+  const trimmed = css.trim();
+  if (!trimmed) return "";
+  if (breakpointTarget === "desktop") {
+    return `@media (min-width: 1025px) {\n${trimmed}\n}`;
+  }
+  if (breakpointTarget === "tablet") {
+    return `@media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1024px) {\n${trimmed}\n}`;
+  }
+  if (breakpointTarget === "mobile") {
+    return `@media (max-width: 767px) {\n${trimmed}\n}`;
+  }
+  return trimmed;
+}

+    legacyFallbacks?: {

+      text?: string | undefined;

+      legacyBorderDisKey?: string;
+      addFocusOutline?: boolean;
+      addInnerTextCascade?: boolean;

+    const stInnerShadowTok = findDesignTokenById(designTokens, stRefs?.innerShadow);

+  );

+import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
 import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
 import { NAV_LINKS } from "./nav-links";
+import {
+  getDiagnosticBreakpoint,
+  getDiagnosticSeverityTier,
+  type CanvasDiagnostic,
+} from "../../lib/ecmdLiveComponentTelemetry";
+
+interface DiagnosticsCounts {
+  totalActive: number;
+  criticalCount: number;
+  desktopCount: number;
+  deferredCount: number;
+  runtimeCount: number;
+  compilerCount: number;
+}
+
+function summarizeDiagnostics(
+  activeList: Array<Partial<CanvasDiagnostic>> | undefined,
+  fallbackTotal = 0,
+  runtimeCount = 0,
+  compilerCount = 0
+): DiagnosticsCounts {
+  if (!Array.isArray(activeList)) {
+    return {
+      totalActive: fallbackTotal + runtimeCount + compilerCount,
+      criticalCount: compilerCount + runtimeCount,
+      desktopCount: fallbackTotal,
+      deferredCount: 0,
+      runtimeCount,
+      compilerCount,
+    };
+  }
+  let criticalCount = compilerCount + runtimeCount;
+  let desktopCount = 0;
+  let deferredCount = 0;
+  for (const item of activeList) {
+    const bp = getDiagnosticBreakpoint(item);
+    const tier = getDiagnosticSeverityTier(item);
+    if (tier === "critical") criticalCount++;
+    if (bp === "desktop") desktopCount++;
+    if (bp !== "desktop" && tier === "layout") deferredCount++;
+  }
+  return {
+    totalActive: activeList.length + runtimeCount + compilerCount,
+    criticalCount,
+    desktopCount,
+    deferredCount,
+    runtimeCount,
+    compilerCount,
+  };
+}
 
 export function AppHeader() {
+  const [diagCounts, setDiagCounts] = useState<DiagnosticsCounts>({
+    totalActive: 0,
+    criticalCount: 0,
+    desktopCount: 0,
+    deferredCount: 0,
+    runtimeCount: 0,
+    compilerCount: 0,
+  });
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!import.meta.env.DEV || typeof window === "undefined") return;
+    let active = true;
+    let latestCanvasList: Array<Partial<CanvasDiagnostic>> = [];
+    let latestRuntimeCount = 0;
+    let latestCompilerCount = 0;
+
+    const syncCounts = () => {
+      if (!active) return;
+      setDiagCounts(
+        summarizeDiagnostics(latestCanvasList, 0, latestRuntimeCount, latestCompilerCount)
+      );
+    };
+
+    const pollDiagnostics = async () => {
+      try {
+        const fetchInit: RequestInit =
+          typeof AbortSignal !== "undefined" && typeof AbortSignal.timeout === "function"
+            ? { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) }
+            : {};
+        const ts = Date.now();
+        const [diagRes, rtRes, telRes] = await Promise.all([
+          fetch("/plasmic-diagnostics.json?t=" + ts, fetchInit).catch(() => null),
+          fetch("/runtime-errors.json?t=" + ts, fetchInit).catch(() => null),
+          fetch("/ai-telemetry.json?t=" + ts, fetchInit).catch(() => null),
+        ]);
+
+        let finalDiagRes = diagRes;
+        if (!finalDiagRes || !finalDiagRes.ok) {
+          finalDiagRes = await fetch("/__ecmd-live-dom?t=" + ts, fetchInit).catch(() => null);
+        }
+
+        if (finalDiagRes && finalDiagRes.ok) {
+          const json = await finalDiagRes.json();
+          const diag =
+            json?.diagnostics && typeof json.diagnostics.totalActive === "number"
+              ? json.diagnostics
+              : json;
+          if (diag && Array.isArray(diag.activeDiagnostics)) {
+            latestCanvasList = diag.activeDiagnostics;
+          }
+        }
+
+        if (rtRes && rtRes.ok) {
+          const rtJson = await rtRes.json();
+          latestRuntimeCount = Array.isArray(rtJson?.errors)
+            ? rtJson.errors.length
+            : Number(rtJson?.totalCount || 0);
+        }
+
+        if (telRes && telRes.ok) {
+          const telJson = await telRes.json();
+          latestCompilerCount = telJson?.tscClean === false ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+
+        syncCounts();
+      } catch {
+        // Silent in dev if diagnostics endpoint is transiently unavailable
+      }
+    };
+
+    pollDiagnostics();
+    const interval = setInterval(pollDiagnostics, 3000);
+    const onDiagUpdated = (e: Event) => {
+      const detail = (e as CustomEvent)?.detail;
+      if (Array.isArray(detail?.activeDiagnostics)) {
+        latestCanvasList = detail.activeDiagnostics;
+        syncCounts();
+      } else {
+        pollDiagnostics();
+      }
+    };
+    const onRuntimeUpdated = (e: Event) => {
+      const detail = (e as CustomEvent)?.detail;
+      if (typeof detail?.totalCount === "number") {
+        latestRuntimeCount = detail.totalCount;
+        syncCounts();
+      } else {
+        pollDiagnostics();
+      }
+    };
+    window.addEventListener("ecmd:diagnostics-updated", onDiagUpdated);
+    window.addEventListener("ecmd:runtime-errors-updated", onRuntimeUpdated);
+
+    return () => {
+      active = false;
+      clearInterval(interval);
+      window.removeEventListener("ecmd:diagnostics-updated", onDiagUpdated);
+      window.removeEventListener("ecmd:runtime-errors-updated", onRuntimeUpdated);
+    };
+  }, []);
+
+  const hasUrgent = diagCounts.criticalCount > 0 || diagCounts.desktopCount > 0;
+  const hasOnlyDeferred = !hasUrgent && diagCounts.deferredCount > 0;
+
+  const pillClass = hasUrgent
+    ? diagCounts.criticalCount > 0
+      ? "border-rose-400/55 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-500/25 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(244,63,94,0.28)]"
+      : "border-amber-400/50 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-500/25 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(245,158,11,0.25)]"
+    : hasOnlyDeferred
+    ? "border-sky-400/45 bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-200 hover:bg-sky-500/25"
+    : "border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-500/20";
+
+  const pillIcon = hasUrgent ? "≡ƒÜ¿" : hasOnlyDeferred ? "≡ƒôÉ" : "Γ£ô";
+  const pillText = hasUrgent
+    ? diagCounts.desktopCount > 0
+      ? `${diagCounts.criticalCount} Critical / ${diagCounts.desktopCount} Desktop`
+      : `${diagCounts.criticalCount} Critical`
+    : hasOnlyDeferred
+    ? `${diagCounts.deferredCount} Mobile/Tablet Queued`
+    : "0 Errors";
+
   return (
-    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-border bg-background/80 backdrop-blur">
-      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-6 px-4 sm:px-6">
-        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
-          <span className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary font-display text-sm font-bold text-primary-foreground shadow-glow">
-            E
-          </span>
-          <span className="font-display text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide">ECMD</span>

 import appCss from "../styles.css?url";
